# 中英文对照 · 实体与字典

> 状态：**生成物**，随代码走。
> **勿手改** —— 由 `node scripts/gen-glossary.mjs` 生成，
> 闸门在 `scripts/check-generated-docs.mjs`（跑一遍产物变不变）。
>
> 两件事放一份：**实体**回答「这张表叫什么、类叫什么、中文叫什么」，
> **字典（取值域）**回答「这一列能存哪些词、每个词中文是什么」。
> 它们是同一套领域词汇的两个切面，分成两份的话改一处必漏另一处。
> 命名的**规定**在 [项目词典](../../requirements/项目词典.md) 与
> [全域命名基准](./全域命名基准.md) —— 那两份说应该是什么，这一份说现在是什么。

### 差集

建表 464 张、挂了实体 371 张。**差集不是缺陷清单** ——
纯关系表、审计表、只被 JdbcClient 读的表本来就不需要 MyBatis 实体。
列在这里是为了让「本该有实体却漏了」这一类看得见（症状是那张表永远读不出来）。

**建了表没有实体（129）**：`core_document_number` · `inv_demand_leaf` · `inv_demand_operation` · `inv_demand_plan` · `inv_event_consumed` · `inv_item_valuation_history` · `inv_outbox_delivery` · > 这一档要当场查：实体指着一张迁移里没有的表，**读写都不会报错，只是永远空**。

---

## 二、字典 / 取值域

同一件事在这个仓库里有三种写法，三处都要在清单里，否则「这一列能存什么」永远只答对三分之一：

| 来源 | 写法 | 数量 |
|---|---|---|
| 后端字符串常量组 | `public static final String X = "X"` | 175 组 / 1059 个取值 |
| 后端 Java enum | `enum X { A, B }` | 220 个 |
| 端上类型 | `type X = "A" \| "B"` 与 `as const` | 260 个 |

**这一节只看得见具名声明。** 裸字面量的取值域（散在 `case` / `Set.of` 里的大写串）
一个都不在上面的数里 —— 它们没有声明处，也就没有名字可登记。
这不是疏漏而是判据的边界：扩到「同一文件里几个大写字面量」会把噪声一起收进来，
而一份混着噪声的清单没人敢信。**这一类只能靠各自的守卫盯**，
例如触达场景码由 `NotifyScene` 收编后，`SceneChannelSeedTest` 两向守着它与种子行。

后端以字符串常量为主是有意的（见 `InvEnums` 的类注释）：库里存的就是这些串，
中间隔一层 enum 的话，反序列化失败报的是「没有这个枚举常量」，
而真正的问题是**库里出现了没人认识的值**。

| `INV_MIRROR_ALLOCATE` | `ALLOCATE` | Paid lifecycle V2: reservation becomes a non-expiring allocation without physical issue. |
| `INV_MIRROR_ISSUE` | `ISSUE` | Fulfillment handoff: only the referenced sub-order lines are issued. |
| `INV_MIRROR_RELEASE_ALLOCATED` | `RELEASE_ALLOCATED` | Refund before handoff releases a paid allocation. |
| `INV_MIRROR_RELEASE` | `RELEASE` | 平台释放 → 进销存撤销预留。 |
| `INV_MIRROR_RESTORE` | `RESTORE` | 售后退回 → 进销存回补库存。 |
| `INV_MIRROR_RESTORE_ORIGINAL` | `RESTORE_ORIGINAL` | 原退款执行恢复：原库存预留事实与目的号决定入库，不重新解析当前配置。 |
| `INV_MIRROR_RETURN_RECEIPT` | `RETURN_RECEIPT` | V2 physical return acceptance with quality routing; independent of cash refund callbacks. |
| `INV_MIRROR_ADJUST` | `ADJUST` | 商家在商品页手改库存 → 进销存跟着改。 |

| `NO_ACCOUNT` | `NO_ACCOUNT` | 上架了但进销存里没有账 —— 多半是投影链路断了，见「链路健康」 |
| `NO_INBOUND` | `NO_INBOUND` | 建了账但一次没进过货：进销存对他来说还只是个空壳 |
| `STALE_LEDGER` | `STALE_LEDGER` | 进过货，但很久没动了 |

错误码（分段见 docs/api/响应格式规范.md §3，与本枚举的同步有守卫测试锁住）。

| 取值 | 参数 | 中文文案 | English | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `PRICING_OWNER_UNSUPPORTED` | `40190, "err.pricing.owner_unsupported"` | 有效的能力或税配置来源暂不可用，请检查配置后重试 | Effective capability or tax configuration source is unavailable; check settings and retry | No authentic capability/tax publication evidence; a saved draft is not permission to activate it. |
| `PRINT_BLOCKED` | `82001, "err.print.blocked"` | 打印操作未完成，请检查配置或任务状态 | Printing could not proceed. Check the configuration or task status. | — |
| `PRINT_CONFLICT` | `82002, "err.print.conflict"` | 打印数据已变化，请刷新后重试 | Print data changed. Refresh before retrying. | — |
| `PRINT_UNAVAILABLE` | `82003, "err.print.unavailable"` | 打印能力暂不可用，请检查配置 | Printing is unavailable. Check the configuration. | — |
| `BAD_REQUEST` | `10400, "err.bad_request"` | 请求参数有误 | Invalid request | — |
| `UNAUTHORIZED` | `10401, "err.unauthorized"` | 请先登录 | Please sign in | — |
| `TOKEN_EXPIRED` | `10402, "err.token_expired"` | 登录已过期，请重新登录 | Your session has expired — please sign in again | 令牌带了、但会话已经不在（过期或被吊销）。 |
| `FORBIDDEN` | `10403, "err.forbidden"` | 没有操作权限 | Permission denied | — |
| `NOT_FOUND` | `10404, "err.not_found"` | 数据不存在 | Not found | — |
| `CONFLICT` | `10409, "err.conflict"` | 资源冲突，请刷新后重试 | Resource conflict | — |
| `TOO_MANY_REQUESTS` | `10429, "err.too_many_requests"` | 操作太频繁，请稍后再试 | Too many requests, please retry later | — |
| `GEO_UNAVAILABLE` | `10503, "err.geo.unavailable"` | 定位取地址未开通 | Address lookup is not enabled | 逆地理编码没配地图厂商密钥：端上据此**藏掉**「定位取地址」按钮，而不是报错 |
| `OTP_TOO_FREQUENT` | `10450, "err.otp.too_frequent"` | 验证码发送过于频繁，请 {0} 秒后重试 | Verification code requested too often. Please retry in {0}s | 发码限流的两条。 |
| `OTP_DAILY_LIMIT` | `10451, "err.otp.daily_limit"` | 今日验证码发送次数已达上限，请明天再试 | Daily verification code limit reached. Please try again tomorrow | — |
| `CAPTCHA_INVALID` | `10452, "err.captcha.invalid"` | 图形验证码错误或已过期，请重新获取 | Captcha is wrong or expired. Please get a new one | 图形验证码错误或已过期。 |
| `RESET_TOKEN_INVALID` | `10453, "err.reset_token.invalid"` | 重置码无效或已过期，请重新申请 | Reset code is invalid or expired. Please request a new one | 密码重置码无效或已过期。 |
| `OTP_LOCKED` | `10454, "err.otp.locked"` | 验证码错误次数过多，请 {0} 分钟后再试 | Too many wrong codes. Please try again in {0} minutes | 验证码连续输错太多次，暂时锁定。 |
| `OTP_INVALID` | `10455, "err.otp.invalid"` | 验证码不对，或已过期 —— 重新获取一次 | Wrong or expired code — request a new one | 验证码不对，或者已经过期。 |
| `PHONE_ALREADY_BOUND` | `10458, "err.phone.already_bound"` | 这个手机号已经绑在你的账号上了 | This phone number is already linked to your account | 这个手机号已经绑在你自己账号上了。 |
| `PHONE_REQUIRED_FOR_PASSWORD` | `10504, "err.password.phone_required"` | 先绑定手机号才能设置登录密码 —— 密码登录要用手机号作为账号 | Link a phone number first. Password sign-in uses your phone number as the account. | 要设登录密码，但这个账号还没绑手机号（C-AC-08）。 |
| `ADDRESS_PHONE_FORMAT` | `10460, "err.address.phone_format"` | 收货人手机号格式不对：中国大陆应为 11 位手机号，其他国家/地区填当地号码（3–20 位数字） | Recipient phone number looks wrong: 11 digits for the Chinese mainland, 3-20 digits elsewhere | 收货人手机号格式不对（V333）。 |
| `OTP_TEST_PHONE_EXISTS_ACCOUNT` | `10461, "err.otp.test_phone_exists_account"` | 这个手机号已经有账号了，不能录进测试号白名单。测试号的用法是先录号、再用它注册，所以录的时候它必须是个没注册过的号 | This number already has an account, so it cannot be added to the test-number list. A test number is meant to be added first and registered afterwards, so it must be an unregistered number | 这个手机号已经有账号了，不许录进白名单。 |
| `OTP_TEST_PHONE_LIMIT` | `10462, "err.otp.test_phone_limit"` | 启用中的测试号已达上限（{0} 个）。先停用或删掉一个不用的，再录新的 | You have reached the limit of {0} active test numbers. Disable or remove one before adding another | 启用中的测试号已达上限。 |
| `OTP_TEST_PHONE_CODE_TOO_SHORT` | `10463, "err.otp.test_phone_code_too_short"` | 固定验证码至少 {0} 位 | The fixed code must be at least {0} digits | 固定验证码太短。 |
| `OTP_TEST_PHONE_FORMAT` | `10464, "err.otp.test_phone_format"` | 请填 11 位大陆手机号 | Enter an 11-digit Chinese mainland mobile number | 手机号格式不对（大陆 11 位）。 |
| `STORE_SLUG_INVALID` | `10465, "err.store.slug_invalid"` | 门店代码只能用 3-32 位小写字母、数字和连字符，首尾不能是连字符 | Store code must be 3-32 lowercase letters, digits or hyphens, and cannot start or end with a hyphen | 门店代码格式不合，或撞了保留词。 |
| `STORE_SLUG_TAKEN` | `10466, "err.store.slug_taken"` | 这个门店代码已经被占用了，换一个 | That store code is already taken — pick another one | 这个门店代码已经被别家店用了。 |
| `PAYOUT_ACCOUNT_NAME_MISMATCH` | `10468, "err.payout_account.name_mismatch"` | 收款账户的户名必须与营业执照的主体名称一致 | The account holder name must match the entity name on the business license | 户名与营业执照主体名不一致。 |
| `PAYOUT_ACCOUNT_PENDING_EXISTS` | `10469, "err.payout_account.pending_exists"` | 已有一张收款账户在审核中，请等审核结果出来再提交 | A payout account is already awaiting review; please wait for the result | 已经有一张卡在等运营核。 |
| `NOT_A_MERCHANT` | `10470, "err.not_a_merchant"` | 你还不是商家，先申请开店再来 | You are not a merchant yet. Apply to open a shop first. | 当前 C 端账号没有关联的商家身份 —— 免登录「切到商家端」时，凭 C 端 user_no 查不到店主的 mch_account。 |
| `PHONE_REQUIRED_FOR_MERCHANT` | `10471, "err.merchant.phone_required"` | 先绑定手机号，绑好就能进你所在的店 | Bind your phone number first, then you can enter your shop. | 切商家端时这个账号还没有已验证的手机号 —— **判不了店员身份**。 |

| `APPLY_NOT_EDITABLE` | `10467, "err.apply.not_editable"` | 这份入驻意向现在改不了，只有待审核时可以修改 | This application can only be edited while it is awaiting review | — |
| `PASSWORD_INVALID` | `10456, "err.password.invalid"` | 手机号或密码不对 | Wrong phone number or password | 手机号或密码不对。 |
| `PASSWORD_NOT_SET` | `10457, "err.password.not_set"` | 这个账号还没设过密码 —— 先用验证码登录，再去「我的」里设置 | No password set for this account — sign in with a code first, then set one under Me | 这个账号还没设过密码。 |
| `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` | `10501, "err.not_implemented"` | 这个功能还没做完，换个方式试试 | This isn't built yet - try another way | 这条路**还没通**，不是参数错了。 |
| `STAFF_SELF_OPERATION` | `10420, "err.staff.self_operation"` | 不能对自己执行该操作 | You cannot perform this action on your own account | 不能停用/降级自己 —— 把自己锁在门外之后只能去库里手改 |

| `COUNT_ALREADY_OPEN` | `20006, "err.trade.count_already_open"` | 已经有一张盘点单开着（{0}），先把它盘完或作废 | A stocktake is already open ({0}); finish or void it first | 已经有一张盘点单开着，不许再开第二张。 |
| `PRICE_CHANGED` | `20002, "err.trade.price_changed"` | 价格已变动，请返回重新下单 | Price changed, please re-submit the order | — |
| `OUT_OF_DELIVERY_RANGE` | `20003, "err.trade.out_of_delivery_range"` | 这个地址超出了商家的自送范围，换个地址或改用其它送货方式 | This address is outside the shop’s delivery range — pick another address or another delivery method | 收货地址超出这家店的自送半径（下单时拦）。 |
| `ORDER_STATE_ILLEGAL` | `20004, "err.trade.order_state_illegal"` | 订单状态不允许该操作 | Operation not allowed in current order state | — |
| `PURCHASE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED` | `20007, "err.trade.purchase_limit_exceeded"` | 超出每人限购，这件商品你还能买 {0} 件 | Purchase limit reached: you can buy {0} more of this item | 超出每人限购（待办设计 P1）。 |
| `STORE_PAUSED` | `20008, "err.trade.store_paused"` | 这家店暂停营业了，去看看同品牌的其他门店 | This store is temporarily closed — try another store of the same brand | 这单要落的门店暂停营业了（READONLY / 平台下线，TDD-C端门店化与门店门户 §2.7）。 |
| `AFTERSALE_FRESH_QUALITY_ONLY` | `20009, "err.trade.aftersale_fresh_quality_only"` | 生鲜发货后只支持损坏/变质/过期等质量问题的退款 | After a fresh item ships, only quality issues (damaged/spoiled/expired) are refundable | 生鲜在已发货 / 已签收后只支持质量原因退款（损坏 / 变质 / 过期）， 非质量原因（不想要等）此时不受理（TDD-售后规则 §2.2）。 |
| `AFTERSALE_PARTIAL_NEED_RECEIPT` | `20010, "err.trade.aftersale_partial_need_receipt"` | 部分退款需等签收后由商家核定金额 | Partial refunds are available only after delivery is confirmed | 部分退款只有已签收才可用（TDD-售后规则 §2.4）：未签收时商家看不到实物损坏比例， 只能全额或驳回，不能核定一个部分金额。 |
| `AFTERSALE_AMOUNT_INVALID` | `20011, "err.trade.aftersale_amount_invalid"` | 退款金额不正确（需大于0且不超过实付） | Invalid refund amount (must be >0 and not exceed the amount paid) | 核定退款额非法：≤0 或超过实付（TDD-售后规则 §2.4）。 |
| `AFTERSALE_WINDOW_CLOSED` | `20012, "err.trade.aftersale_window_closed"` | 已超过售后期，不能再申请 | The after-sales period has ended | 已过售后期（订单完成后 7 天，质量类 15 天。 |
| `AFTERSALE_CHANNEL_PARTIAL_UNSUPPORTED` | `20013, "err.trade.aftersale_channel_partial_unsupported"` | 这笔订单的付款方式不支持部分退款，可改用积分补偿或线下退款 | This payment method does not support partial refunds; use points compensation or an offline refund instead | 本单支付渠道不支持部分退款（或已超次数 / 期限）—— 改用积分补偿、线下退款登记或整单退。 |
| `ORDER_POINTS_MANUAL_REQUIRED` | `20014, "err.trade.order_points_manual_required"` | 该订单包含历史积分，请联系平台人工处理 | This order contains historical points. Contact the platform for manual resolution. | Do not report cash-order cleanup complete when an older order still contains points liabilities. |
| `ALREADY_VERIFIED` | `30001, "err.fulfillment.already_verified"` | 该取货码已核销 | This pickup code has been used | — |
| `NOT_THIS_PICKUP_POINT` | `30002, "err.fulfillment.not_this_pickup"` | 该订单不属于本自提点 | Order does not belong to this pickup point | — |
| `ORDER_REFUNDED` | `30003, "err.fulfillment.order_refunded"` | 订单已退款，不可核销 | Order refunded, cannot be picked up | — |
| `BATCH_TRANSITION_ILLEGAL` | `30004, "err.fulfillment.batch_transition_illegal"` | 批次状态只能按「计划→发车→到货→签收」逐步推进，不能跳步 | A batch moves one step at a time: planned, dispatched, arrived, signed | 批次跳步（没发车就到货、没到货就签收）。 |
| `OVERDUE_GRACE_TOO_SHORT` | `30005, "err.fulfillment.overdue_grace_too_short"` | 宽限期不能少于 1 小时 | The grace period cannot be under 1 hour | 逾期宽限期不足 1 小时。 |
| `WAYBILL_LOCKED` | `30006, "err.fulfillment.waybill_locked"` | 已签收的快递单不能改运单号 | A delivered shipment's waybill number cannot be changed | 已签收的快递单不许改运单号 —— 等于把一条已完成的轨迹指向别处 |
| `WAYBILL_DUPLICATED` | `30007, "err.fulfillment.waybill_duplicated"` | 该承运商下这个运单号已被别的单占用 | Another shipment already uses that waybill number with this carrier | 同一承运商下运单号重复 —— 会把两单的轨迹搅在一起 |
| `FREIGHT_DEFAULT_LOCKED` | `30008, "err.fulfillment.freight_default_locked"` | 默认模板不能归档 —— 归档之后新商家没有模板可用 | The default template cannot be archived — new merchants would have none to use | 默认运费模板不能归档 —— 归档之后新商家没有模板可用 |
| `CARRIER_PRIORITY_TAKEN` | `30009, "err.fulfillment.carrier_priority_taken"` | 该优先级已被别的运力占用，同优先级时选哪家将取决于顺序 | That priority is taken by another carrier — sharing one means the winner depends on order | 运力优先级重复 —— 同优先级时选哪家取决于查询顺序，那是隐性行为 |
| `CARRIER_KEY_MISSING` | `30010, "err.fulfillment.carrier_key_missing"` | 该运力尚未配置接入密钥，启用后下单会当场失败 | This carrier has no API key configured — bookings would fail the moment it is enabled | 没配接入密钥不能启用 —— 启用后下单当场失败，比不启用更糟 |
| `CARRIER_HAS_IN_FLIGHT` | `30011, "err.fulfillment.carrier_has_in_flight"` | 该运力还有在途快递单，停用后这些单的轨迹会拉不回来 | This carrier still has parcels in transit — disabling it strands their tracking | 还有在途快递单，停用后那些单的轨迹拉不回来 |
| `CARRIER_LAST_ENABLED` | `30012, "err.fulfillment.carrier_last_enabled"` | 至少要保留一家启用的运力 —— 全停之后快递单无处可下 | Keep at least one carrier enabled — with none there is nowhere to book a parcel | 不能停掉最后一家启用的运力 —— 全停之后快递单无处可下 |
| `WAYBILL_SUBSCRIBE_LIMIT` | `30013, "err.fulfillment.waybill_subscribe_limit"` | 这个运单号本月在快递100 已订阅 4 次，不能再订阅；可换其他渠道或下月再试 | This waybill has been subscribed on Kuaidi100 4 times this month; use another channel or try next month | 快递100 同一公司 + 单号每月最多订阅 4 次，这一单本月用完了（TDD-物流模块 O3）。 |
| `WAYBILL_TERMINAL` | `30014, "err.fulfillment.waybill_terminal"` | 运单已签收或已作废，不需要重放 | The shipment is already signed for or cancelled; nothing to replay | 运单已签收或已作废，重放没有意义 |
| `LOGISTICS_CHANNEL_UNAVAILABLE` | `30015, "err.fulfillment.logistics_channel_unavailable"` | 这个物流渠道没有启用或凭据没配，不能用它订阅：{0} | This logistics channel is disabled or missing credentials, so it can't subscribe: {0} | 指定的物流渠道没启用或凭据没配。 |
| `COUPON_SOLD_OUT` | `40001, "err.marketing.coupon_sold_out"` | 优惠券已领完 | Coupon sold out | — |
| `COUPON_NOT_APPLICABLE` | `40002, "err.marketing.coupon_not_applicable"` | 该券不适用于当前订单 | Coupon not applicable to this order | — |
| `COUPON_DISCOUNT_CAP_REQUIRED` | `40003, "err.marketing.coupon_discount_cap_required"` | 折扣券必须设置封顶金额 | Discount coupons must have a maximum discount cap | 折扣券建券/改券时必须封顶——取消「0 = 不封顶」，见 TDD-营销预算前置 |
| `COUPON_TOTAL_COUNT_REQUIRED` | `40004, "err.marketing.coupon_total_count_required"` | 发行量必须大于 0 | Total count must be greater than 0 | 发行量必须 >0——不限量券的敞口同样算不出来 |
| `COUPON_BUDGET_BELOW_EXPOSURE` | `40005, "err.marketing.coupon_budget_below_exposure"` | 预算不能低于最大敞口（{0} 分） | Budget cannot be below the maximum exposure ({0} minor units) | 预算低于「发行量 × 单张最大优惠」——不接受一个从第一天起就不可能满足的预算 |
| `ATTRIBUTION_PRIORITY_INVALID` | `40006, "err.marketing.attribution_priority_invalid"` | 归因优先级必须覆盖全部 {0} 个来源且不重复 | Attribution priority must cover all {0} sources with no repeats | 归因优先级不是全序（三个来源有重复或有遗漏）——半张表在冲突时会随机裁决 |
| `ATTRIBUTION_WINDOW_INVALID` | `40007, "err.marketing.attribution_window_invalid"` | 归因窗口期需在 {0}–{1} 天之间（0 天等于关掉归因） | The attribution window must be {0}–{1} days (0 days switches attribution off) | 归因窗口期越界（1–90 天）。 |
| `ATTRIBUTION_FACTOR_REQUIRED` | `40008, "err.marketing.attribution_factor_required"` | 新客判定至少要选一个因子（设备 / 手机号） | New-customer detection needs at least one signal (device or phone number) | 新客判定一个因子都没选 = 所有人都是新客，新人券会被无限领 |
| `FISSION_REWARD_MUST_BE_COUPON` | `40009, "err.marketing.fission_reward_must_be_coupon"` | 邀请有礼只能发券，不能发现金 | Referral rewards are coupons only, never cash | 邀请有礼只能发券（ADR-004：去团长化后不存在现金激励） |
| `FISSION_REWARD_COUNT_INVALID` | `40010, "err.marketing.fission_reward_count_invalid"` | 邀请人与被邀请人至少一方有奖励，且张数不能为负 | At least one side has to get something, and counts cannot be negative | 两边都是 0 张 = 一个不发奖的裂变活动，或者张数为负 |
| `COUPON_RATE_INVALID` | `40011, "err.marketing.coupon_rate_invalid"` | 折扣要填万分比，如 8500 表示八五折 | Enter the discount in basis points, e.g. 8500 for 15% off | 折扣填成了百分数（88）而不是万分比（8800）。 |
| `COUPON_SCOPE_UNSUPPORTED` | `40012, "err.marketing.coupon_scope_unsupported"` | 下单抵扣的券暂不支持按类目或商品限定，可改成到店核销 | Checkout coupons cannot be limited to categories or items yet; use in-store redemption | 下单抵扣的券不支持按类目/商品限定范围。 |
| `COUPON_TOTAL_BELOW_ISSUED` | `40013, "err.marketing.coupon_total_below_issued"` | 发行量不能低于已领张数 | Issue count cannot be lower than the number already claimed | 发行量改到低于已领张数 = 人为造出一个「已经超发了」的状态，没有补救动作 |
| `COUPON_NOT_ACTIVE` | `40014, "err.marketing.coupon_not_active"` | 这张券已暂停或已结束，发不出去 | This coupon is paused or ended and cannot be issued | 券已暂停或已结束，发不出去。 |
| `COUPON_BUDGET_EXCEEDED` | `40015, "err.marketing.coupon_budget_exceeded"` | 超出剩余预算，整批未发放 | Over the remaining budget; nothing was issued | 本批敞口超出剩余预算。 |
| `COUPON_CODE_NOT_FOUND` | `40016, "err.marketing.coupon_code_not_found"` | 没找到这张券，确认一下码有没有输错 | No coupon found for this code | 核销码在这家店查不到。 |
| `COUPON_REDEEM_CONFLICT` | `40017, "err.marketing.coupon_redeem_conflict"` | 这张券刚被核销过，请刷新后再看 | This coupon was just redeemed; refresh to see the latest | 这一次核销没抢到（带条件的 UPDATE 影响 0 行）。 |
| `ACTIVITY_ALWAYS_ON_NEEDS_CAP` | `40018, "err.marketing.activity_always_on_needs_cap"` | 长期活动必须设限量或预算，否则没有停下来的那一天 | An always-on activity needs a quota or budget, or it never stops | 长期活动没有限量也没有预算 = 永久敞口。 |
| `ACTIVITY_QUOTA_REQUIRED` | `40019, "err.marketing.activity_quota_required"` | 改价和送商品的活动必须设限量 | Price and gift activities need a quota | 改单价 / 送商品必须限量：单次成本由商品决定，不设上限时卖得越好亏得越多 |
| `ACTIVITY_GOODS_REQUIRED` | `40020, "err.marketing.activity_goods_required"` | 请选择参加活动的商品 | Pick the products this activity applies to | 改单价 / 送商品必须选商品。 |
| `ACTIVITY_RECURRING_RULE_INVALID` | `40021, "err.marketing.activity_recurring_rule_invalid"` | 请设置周期规则（周几、几点到几点） | Set the recurring rule (days and hours) | 周期规则读不出来。 |
| `ACTIVITY_QUOTA_BELOW_USED` | `40022, "err.marketing.activity_quota_below_used"` | 限量不能低于已用数量 | Quota cannot be lower than what is already used | 限量改到低于已用。 |
| `ACTIVITY_ENDED_IMMUTABLE` | `40023, "err.marketing.activity_ended_immutable"` | 已结束的活动不能修改或重新开启，请复制一个新的 | Ended activities cannot be edited or restarted; copy it into a new one | 已结束的活动不能改、也不能复活。 |
| `PERIOD_CUT_OFF` | `40024, "err.marketing.period_cut_off"` | 本期已截单，不能取消 | This batch has closed and can no longer be cancelled | 集单已截单，买家不能再撤单 —— 商家已经按这一期的量去采购了 |
| `PERIOD_STATE_CONFLICT` | `40025, "err.marketing.period_state_conflict"` | 本期状态已变化，请刷新后再试 | This batch has changed; refresh and try again | 对不在那个状态的期做了动作：提前截单只能对收单中的期，处理只能对未达起订量的期 |
| `PERIOD_MIXED` | `40026, "err.marketing.period_mixed"` | 不同截单时间的商品请分开下单 | Items with different cut-off times must be ordered separately | 一张子单里有两个不同集单活动的货：一单只能挂一期，否则其中一期的汇总会少掉这几件 |
| `PERIOD_FULL` | `40027, "err.marketing.period_full"` | 本期份数已满，请下一期再来 | This batch is full; please order in the next one | 这一期的份数已满 |
| `GOODS_IN_PRESALE` | `40028, "err.marketing.goods_in_presale"` | 预售中的商品不能参加集单 | Items on pre-sale cannot join a group order batch | 预售中的商品不能加进集单：两套截单与到货口径叠在一件货上，买家看到的是两个日子 |
| `ACTIVITY_RULE_LOCKED` | `40029, "err.marketing.activity_rule_locked"` | 活动已开始，只能改结束时间和上限；改规则请结束后另建 | This activity has started. Only the end time and caps can change; end it and create a new one to change the rules | 开始了的活动改了规则（门槛、优惠、商品、人群、排期……）。 |
| `GROUP_CLOSED` | `40030, "err.marketing.group_closed"` | 团已成团、已散或已过期，不能再参团 | This group has closed and can no longer be joined | 参团时团已成、已散或已过期 —— 下单那一刻判，付款前买家就知道 |
| `GROUP_JOIN_NEEDS_UPGRADE` | `40033, "err.marketing.group_join_needs_upgrade"` | 参团改为下单付款，请升级到最新版本 | Joining a group now goes through checkout. Please update the app | 旧版 C 端还在调「直接参团」。 |
| `ACTIVITY_RISK_UNCONFIRMED` | `40034, "err.marketing.activity_risk_unconfirmed"` | 这个活动常驻、无门槛，每一单都会减，请确认后再保存 | This offer is always on with no minimum spend; every order gets the discount. Please confirm before saving | 常驻 + 无门槛 + 直减的活动保存前要商家确认一次（待办设计 P7）。 |
| `ACTIVITY_CHOICE_UNAVAILABLE` | `40035, "err.marketing.activity_choice_unavailable"` | 你选的活动已经不可用了，请重新选择优惠 | The offer you chose is no longer available. Please choose again | 顾客在下单页选的活动这一刻不成立了（结束了、配额刚用完、门槛不够了）。 |
| `ENROLLMENT_CLOSED` | `40031, "err.marketing.enrollment_closed"` | 报名已截止或不满足报名门槛 | Enrollment has closed, or the store does not meet the requirements | 报名不成：过了报名截止，或不满足报名门槛（评分、违规、类目） |
| `ENROLLMENT_OVER_BUDGET` | `40032, "err.marketing.enrollment_over_budget"` | 通过这份报名会超出平台预算 | Approving this enrollment would exceed the platform budget | 通过这份报名会超出平台预算。 |
| `ENTITLEMENT_FEATURE_UNAVAILABLE` | `40040, "err.entitlement.feature_unavailable"` | 此权益功能暂未开放，请稍后再试 | This benefits feature is not available yet. Please try again later. | A disabled new rights workflow is not a missing staff permission and must not trigger a login redirect. |
| `MEMBER_CODE_INVALID` | `40041, "err.member.code_invalid"` | 会员码无效或已过期，请刷新后重试 | Member code is invalid or expired. Refresh and try again. | — |
| `SPLIT_RECEIVER_NOT_READY` | `50001, "err.settle.receiver_not_ready"` | 商家收款账户未报备 | Merchant payout account not ready | — |
| `SPLIT_EXPIRED` | `50002, "err.settle.split_expired"` | 分账已超期 | Profit sharing expired | — |
| `WITHDRAW_STATE_ILLEGAL` | `50003, "err.settle.withdraw_state_illegal"` | 该提现单当前状态不允许此操作，请刷新后重试 | This withdrawal cannot be handled in its current state — refresh and retry | 提现单状态不允许该动作（P-12.2.1）。 |
| `WITHDRAW_OVER_BALANCE` | `50004, "err.settle.withdraw_over_balance"` | 申请金额超过申请时的可提余额 | The amount exceeds the available balance recorded when it was requested | 申请金额超过申请时的可提余额快照。 |
| `WITHDRAW_BELOW_MIN` | `50005, "err.settle.withdraw_below_min"` | 单笔提现金额低于下限 | The amount is below the minimum for a single withdrawal | 低于单笔下限 —— 渠道手续费比本金还贵。 |
| `WITHDRAW_REVIEW_NOTE_REQUIRED` | `50006, "err.settle.withdraw_review_note_required"` | 大额提现必须填写复核说明 | A review note is required for a large withdrawal | 超过复核阈值却没写复核说明。 |
| `WITHDRAW_MERCHANT_BANNED` | `50010, "err.settle.withdraw_merchant_banned"` | 该商家处于封禁中，请先解封再处理提现 | The merchant is banned — lift the ban before handling the withdrawal | 商家处于封禁中，不放行提现。 |
| `WITHDRAW_PENDING_EXISTS` | `50011, "err.settle.withdraw_pending_exists"` | 还有一笔提现在处理中，完成后才能再提 | A withdrawal is still being processed — finish that one first | 上一笔提现还没走完。 |
| `WITHDRAW_OVER_WITHDRAWABLE` | `50012, "err.settle.withdraw_over_withdrawable"` | 可提金额不足：当前可提 {0} 元 | Not enough withdrawable balance: {0} yuan available right now | 申请额超过当前可提。 |
| `INVOICE_OVER_SETTLED` | `50007, "err.settle.invoice_over_settled"` | 开票金额超过该周期已结算金额 | The invoice exceeds what was settled in this period | 开票金额超过该周期已结算金额。 |
| `INVOICE_TAX_NO_REQUIRED` | `50008, "err.settle.invoice_tax_no_required"` | 企业抬头必须填写纳税人识别号 | A company title needs a tax ID | 企业抬头缺纳税人识别号 —— 开出来对方也入不了账。 |
| `TAX_RATE_TOO_HIGH` | `50009, "err.settle.tax_rate_too_high"` | 个税税率超过上限，请核对后再保存 | The tax rate is above the allowed cap — check it before saving | 个税税率超过硬上限（45%）。 |
| `POINTS_SELF_REVIEW` | `50040, "err.points.self_review"` | 申请人不能复核自己的资金申请，请由另一名有权限的人员处理。 | The maker cannot review their own treasury request. Ask another authorized checker. | Points operator requests retain the original financial reference when a check rejects them. |
| `POINTS_REVISION_CHANGED` | `50041, "err.points.revision_changed"` | 积分记录或请求内容已变化，请刷新核对并保留原资金指令号。 | The points record or request changed. Reload and retain the original funds ticket. | — |
| `POINTS_FUNDS_UNAVAILABLE` | `50042, "err.points.funds_unavailable"` | 可用资金不足或当前通道能力未开启，请核对资金占用和通道配置。 | Available funds are insufficient or the channel capability is disabled. Check locks and channel configuration. | — |
| `POINTS_EVIDENCE_REQUIRED` | `50043, "err.points.evidence_required"` | 资金证据未通过核验，请核对原回执；声明回执不代表已到账。 | Funds evidence was not verified. Check the original receipt; declaring one does not confirm arrival. | — |

| `RISK_EVENT_HANDLED` | `60002, "err.risk.event_handled"` | 该事件已处置，请刷新列表 | This event has already been handled — refresh the list | 事件已被处置（多半是同事先动了手）。 |
| `RISK_VERDICT_REQUIRED` | `60003, "err.risk.verdict_required"` | 处置结论必填：确认与排除都要写清依据 | A conclusion is required — say what it rests on, whether you confirm or dismiss | 处置结论必填。 |
| `BLACKLIST_REASON_REQUIRED` | `60004, "err.risk.blacklist_reason_required"` | 拉黑原因必填：申诉时要能看到自己因为什么被拉黑 | A reason is required — on appeal they have to be able to see what it was for | 拉黑原因必填：申诉时被拉黑者要能看到自己因为什么被拉黑 |
| `BLACKLIST_UNTIL_REQUIRED` | `60005, "err.risk.blacklist_until_required"` | 到期时间必填，且必须晚于当前时间——不允许无期限拉黑 | An expiry is required and must be in the future — no open-ended blocks | 到期时间必填且必须在未来。 |
| `BLACKLIST_DUPLICATE` | `60006, "err.risk.blacklist_duplicate"` | {0} 已在生效中的黑名单里 | {0} is already on the active blocklist | 该对象已在生效中的黑名单里——重复拉黑会让「解禁」变成要点两次的迷题 |
| `BLACKLIST_NO_APPEAL` | `60007, "err.risk.blacklist_no_appeal"` | 该记录没有待裁决的申诉 | This entry has no appeal awaiting a ruling | 该记录没有待裁决的申诉 |
| `RISK_THRESHOLD_INVALID` | `60008, "err.risk.threshold_invalid"` | 触发阈值必须大于 0（0 等于全量拦截） | The threshold must be above 0 — at 0 it blocks everything | 触发阈值必须 &gt; 0——0 等于全量拦截，而页面上它只是一个普通数字 |

| `PAY_CHECKOUT_EXPIRED` | `70093, "err.pay.checkout_expired"` | 收款链接已失效，请向商家重新获取 | This payment link is no longer valid. Please ask the merchant for a new one | 收银令牌过期、已作废或支付单已关（安全规范 §2.5：除「不存在」外只回这一种） |
| `PAY_CHECKOUT_LIMIT` | `70094, "err.pay.checkout_limit"` | 金额超过收款码的限额 | The amount exceeds the limit of this payment code | 静态码超单笔上限或单码日累计上限 |
| `PAY_OPEN_TIMESTAMP_EXPIRED` | `70095, "err.pay.open_timestamp_expired"` | 请求时间戳超出允许范围，请校准时钟后重试 | The request timestamp is outside the allowed window; sync your clock and retry | 时间戳在 ±300 秒窗口外：对方时钟偏了 |
| `PAY_OPEN_REPLAYED` | `70096, "err.pay.open_replayed"` | 请求已处理过，请换一个 nonce 再发 | This request was already processed; send it again with a new nonce | nonce 在窗口内用过：重放，或调用方重试时没换 nonce |
| `PAY_OPEN_FIELD_ENCRYPT_REQUIRED` | `70097, "err.pay.open_field_encrypt_required"` | 敏感字段须加密后传入 | Sensitive fields must be encrypted | 标了 x-sensitive 的字段传了明文 |
| `PAY_OPEN_FIELD_DECRYPT_FAILED` | `70098, "err.pay.open_field_decrypt_failed"` | 加密字段无法解密，请检查加密密钥与参数 | An encrypted field could not be decrypted; check the encryption key and parameters | 加密字段解不开（版本不认识 / 标签不符 / OAEP 参数错 —— 不分哪一步） |
| `PAY_KEY_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE` | `70099, "err.pay.key_service_unavailable"` | 密钥服务暂不可用，请稍后重试 | The key service is temporarily unavailable; please retry later | 外部密钥服务（KMS / HSM）不可用 |
| `EXPRESS_CHANNEL_OFF` | `70070, "err.express.channel_off"` | 平台快递代下单还没开通，请先自己叫快递并填写运单号 | Courier booking is not available yet. Book a courier yourself and enter the tracking number | 快递代下单（TDD-快递100商家寄件）。 |
| `EXPRESS_PICKUP_EXISTS` | `70071, "err.express.pickup_exists"` | 这一单已经叫过快递，取消后才能重新叫 | A courier is already booked for this order. Cancel it before booking again | — |
| `EXPRESS_SENDER_INCOMPLETE` | `70072, "err.express.sender_incomplete"` | 门店寄件信息不全：请在店铺设置里填好门店地址，并确认店主账号绑定了手机号 | Store sender details are incomplete: set the store address in shop settings and make sure the owner account has a phone number | — |
| `EXPRESS_PROVIDER_REJECTED` | `70073, "err.express.provider_rejected"` | 快递公司没有接单：{0} | The courier declined this order: {0} | — |
| `EXPRESS_NOT_CANCELLABLE` | `70074, "err.express.not_cancellable"` | 快递员已取件，不能再取消 | The parcel has been picked up and can no longer be cancelled | — |
| `PAY_CHANNEL_UNAVAILABLE` | `70045, "err.pay.channel_unavailable"` | 收款通道还没接通，请联系平台开通后再提交（不是你填的资料有问题） | Payouts are not connected yet — contact the platform. Nothing is wrong with what you filled in. | 这个支付通道还没接通 —— 不是他填错了什么。 |
| `PROXY_ORDER_AMOUNT_LIMIT` | `70046, "err.trade.proxy_amount_limit"` | 这一单金额超过代客下单的单笔上限，请让顾客自行下单 | This order exceeds the per-order cap for placing an order on a customer’s behalf — ask the customer to place it themselves | 代客下单的两条限额（M6）。 |
| `PROXY_ORDER_DAILY_LIMIT` | `70047, "err.trade.proxy_daily_limit"` | 今天的代客下单笔数已达上限，明天再试或联系管理员调整 | You have reached today’s limit for orders placed on a customer’s behalf — try tomorrow or ask an administrator to raise it | — |
| `FULFILLMENT_TIER_DENIED` | `70024, "err.trade.fulfillment_tier_denied"` | 该商家的主体类型暂不支持此配送方式，请改用自提或快递 | This merchant’s entity type cannot use that delivery method. Please choose pickup or courier. | — |
| `MERCHANT_QUOTA_EXHAUSTED` | `70019, "err.trade.merchant_quota_exhausted"` | 该商家本期收款额度已用尽，暂时无法下单 | This merchant has used up its collection quota for the period. | 该商家的收款额度已用尽。 |
| `INVOICE_REQUIRED` | `70026, "err.settle.invoice_required"` | 进项票未核验通过，请先核验发票再登记付款 | The purchase invoice is not verified yet — verify it before recording payment | 进项票未核验通过，不允许登记付款（**票到付款**）。 |
| `BATCH_NOT_RELEASABLE` | `70077, "err.settle.batch_not_releasable"` | 批次当前状态 {0} 不能放款，只有自查通过（RECONCILED）的批次能放 | Batch is in status {0} and cannot be released; only RECONCILED batches can | 放款：批次不在可放款态（只有 RECONCILED 能放。 |
| `PAYOUT_ACCOUNT_MISSING` | `70078, "err.settle.payout_account_missing"` | 主体 {0} 没有生效中的收款账户，钱不知道打给谁 | Entity {0} has no active payout account — nowhere to send the money | 放款：主体没有生效中的收款账户，钱不知道打给谁 |
| `PAYOUT_NOT_PAYABLE` | `70079, "err.settle.payout_not_payable"` | 放款记录当前状态 {0}，不能登记付款或退回 | Payout is in status {0}; it cannot be marked paid or failed | 放款记录不在能登记付款 / 退回的状态 |
| `PAYOUT_INVOICE_PENDING` | `70080, "err.settle.payout_invoice_pending"` | 本批有结算单的进项票还没了结，先核验或标无票：{0} | Some bills in this batch still have unsettled purchase invoices — verify them or mark invoice-exempt: {0} | 放款：本批有结算单的进项票没了结，{0} 是单号清单。 |
| `INVOICE_AMOUNT_MISMATCH` | `70016, "err.settle.invoice_amount_mismatch"` | 发票金额与应付合计不符，请检查开票周期是否选错 | Invoice amount does not match the payable total — check the billing period | 发票金额与应付合计不符。 |
| `INVOICE_TITLE_MISMATCH` | `70017, "err.settle.invoice_title_mismatch"` | 开票方名称与主体名称不一致，请核对后重开 | The invoice issuer name does not match the supplier entity name | 开票方名称与供应商主体名不一致 —— 三流不一致会被认定虚开风险 |
| `FLASH_MULTI_SKU_UNSUPPORTED` | `70004, "err.goods.flash_multi_sku"` | 这件商品有多个规格，而限时特价只能设一个价——请改用满减或买赠 | This item has multiple specs but a flash campaign carries a single price — use spend-and-save or buy-and-gift instead | 多规格商品不支持商品级限时特价。 |
| `CAMPAIGN_STORE_UNSUPPORTED` | `70005, "err.campaign.store_unsupported"` | 只有满减能限定门店——限时特价与买赠会改商品页的价格和赠品标，而顾客浏览时还没选自提点 | Only spend-and-save can target a single store — flash and buy-and-gift change what the product page shows, and the pickup point is not chosen yet at that moment | 这类活动不支持限定门店。 |
| `BIZ_ROLE_FORBIDDEN` | `70006, "err.biz.role_forbidden"` | 这一步需要「{0}」的权限，你在这家店的角色没有——让店主给你加个角色 | This needs the “{0}” permission, which your role at this store does not have — ask the owner to grant you a role | 这个角色不能做这件事（B 端）。 |
| `SERVICE_SCOPE_NOT_ALLOWED` | `70018, "err.merchant.service_scope_not_allowed"` | 当前不支持这个经营范围，请重新选择 | That service scope is not available — please pick another | 经营范围不是合法取值，或这一期没开放这一档（sys_setting 的 merchant.service-scope-enabled）。 |
| `STORE_QUOTA_EXCEEDED` | `70020, "err.merchant.store_quota_exceeded"` | 门店数量已达当前套餐上限（已开 {0} 家，上限 {1} 家，当前 {2} 版）。可停用一家旧店，或升级套餐 | You have {0} stores, and the {2} plan allows {1}. Disable one, or upgrade your plan. | 门店数量已到套餐上限。 |
| `STORE_SUSPENDED_BY_PLATFORM` | `70021, "err.merchant.store_suspended_by_platform"` | 该门店已被平台停业处置，如有疑问请联系平台客服 | This store has been suspended by the platform. Please contact platform support. | 门店被平台强制下线，商家的启停操作一律拒绝。 |
| `STAFF_QUOTA_EXCEEDED` | `70022, "err.merchant.staff_quota_exceeded"` | 子账号数量已达当前套餐上限（已有 {0} 个，上限 {1} 个，当前 {2} 版）。可停用一个旧账号，或升级套餐 | You have {0} sub-accounts, and the {2} plan allows {1}. Disable one, or upgrade your plan. | 子账号数量已达套餐上限（P-11.2）。 |
| `PLAN_CAPABILITY_REQUIRED` | `70023, "err.merchant.plan_capability_required"` | 跨店总览与对比是成长版 / 连锁版的能力，当前是 {0} 版。升级套餐后，多家店的今日订单、销售额与待办就能并排看 | Cross-store overview and comparison come with the PRO and CHAIN plans; you are on {0}. Upgrade to see every store side by side. | 当前档位没有这项能力位（一期只有 cross_store_stats，B-11.12.5/6）。 |
| `CATEGORY_TOO_DEEP` | `80001, "err.category.too_deep"` | 类目最多两级，二级之下不能再建子类目 | Categories go two levels deep; a second-level category cannot take children | 类目最多两级（V168 由三级收敛）—— 端上的选择器只渲染两层，更深的节点查得到、选不到。 |
| `CATEGORY_IN_USE` | `80002, "err.category.in_use"` | 该类目下还有商品或子类目，不能归档 | This category still holds goods or sub-categories and cannot be archived | 下面还挂着商品或未归档的子类目 —— 直接归档会让那些商品挂在一个不存在的类目上。 |
| `CATEGORY_PARENT_ARCHIVED` | `80003, "err.category.parent_archived"` | 上级类目已归档，请先恢复上级类目 | The parent category is archived; restore it first | 父类目已归档，恢复它会造出一个挂在已删父节点下的孤儿。 |
| `CATEGORY_NOT_FOUND` | `80007, "err.category.not_found"` | 所选类目不存在，请重新选择 | That category does not exist. Please pick another one | 建品时传了一个查无此项的类目编号。 |
| `STORE_CATEGORY_IN_USE` | `80008, "err.store_category.in_use"` | 该分类下还有商品，请先把它们移到别的分类 | This shelf still has products — move them elsewhere first | 删一个底下还有商品的门店经营类目。 |
| `CATEGORY_HAS_NO_SPEC` | `80010, "err.category.has_no_spec"` | 这个类目还没配规格，启用后商家建品会拿不到规格 —— 请先在「类目规格」里给它配上 | This category has no spec dimensions yet; merchants would get none when listing goods. Configure them first. | 启用一个**没配规格**的二级类目。 |
| `PAY_MODE_NOT_SUPPORTED` | `80011, "err.pay_mode.not_supported"` | 这件商品不支持所选的支付方式 | This item does not support the selected payment method | 所选支付方式这件商品不支持。 |
| `OFFLINE_PAY_NOT_QUALIFIED` | `80012, "err.offline_pay.not_qualified"` | 该商家暂不具备线下收款资质，请选择线上支付 | This merchant is not yet qualified for offline payment — please pay online | 主体没有有效资质，不能线下收款。 |
| `PLATFORM_COUPON_OFFLINE_FORBIDDEN` | `80013, "err.coupon.platform_offline_forbidden"` | 平台优惠券不支持线下支付，可改用线上支付或取消该券 | Platform coupons cannot be used with offline payment | 线下支付不能用平台券。 |
| `APPOINTMENT_SLOT_FULL` | `80014, "err.appointment.slot_full"` | 该时段已约满，请换一个时段 | That time slot is fully booked — please pick another | 这个时段已经约满。 |
| `APPOINTMENT_SLOT_UNAVAILABLE` | `80015, "err.appointment.slot_unavailable"` | 该时段暂未开放预约 | That time slot is not open for booking | 这个时段未开放或已停约。 |
| `SPEC_IN_USE` | `80016, "err.spec.in_use"` | 仍有 {0} 件商品使用该规格，请先调整那些商品或将其下架，再停用 | Still used by {0} product(s). Adjust or delist them before archiving | 停用/归档一个还有商品在用的规格值或维度。 |
| `GOODS_SPEC_UNRESOLVED` | `80017, "err.goods.spec_unresolved"` | 以下规格档位已停用或不存在，请调整商品规格后再上架：{0} | These spec options are archived or missing. Adjust the product before publishing: {0} | 上架编译点：草稿引用的规格档位已停用/合并/不存在。 |
| `GOODS_COVER_REQUIRED` | `80019, "err.goods.cover_required"` | 提交审核前要先上传主图 | Upload a cover image before submitting for review | 提审时主图为空 |
| `GOODS_TITLE_TOO_SHORT` | `80020, "err.goods.title_too_short"` | 标题至少 {0} 个字 | The title needs at least {0} characters | 标题短于平台下限，参数是下限字数 |
| `GOODS_TITLE_TOO_LONG` | `80021, "err.goods.title_too_long"` | 标题最多 {0} 个字 | The title can be at most {0} characters | 标题超过平台上限，参数是上限字数 |
| `GOODS_TITLE_BANNED_WORD` | `80022, "err.goods.title_banned_word"` | 标题里的「{0}」不能用 | The word "{0}" cannot be used in the title | 标题命中禁售词，参数是命中的那个词 |
| `GOODS_TITLE_BANNED_WORD_REASON` | `80023, "err.goods.title_banned_word_reason"` | 标题里的「{0}」不能用：{1} | The word "{0}" cannot be used in the title: {1} | 标题命中禁售词，且词表里给了原因（参数：词、原因）。 |
| `GOODS_DRAFT_STALE` | `80018, "err.goods.draft_stale"` | 线上版本在你编辑期间已被修改，请查看差异后重新提交 | The live version changed while you were editing. Review the differences and resubmit | 草稿基于的线上版本已被别人改过（多端编辑/运营强改）。 |
| `PRESALE_CUTOFF_AFTER_ARRIVAL` | `80004, "err.presale.cutoff_after_arrival"` | 截单时间必须早于到货时间，否则货到了还能继续下单 | The cut-off must come before the arrival time, or people keep ordering after the goods land | 截单时间不早于到货时间（P-3.3.2）。 |
| `SPEC_TEMPLATE_CODE_REQUIRED` | `80005, "err.spec_template.code_required"` | 平台模板的每个选项都必须填规格编码，否则各店的写法聚合不到一起 | Every option in a platform template needs an option code, otherwise the wording each shop uses cannot be grouped | 平台规格模板的选项缺 code（P-3.4 / B-4.5）。 |
| `SPEC_TEMPLATE_DUPLICATE` | `80006, "err.spec_template.duplicate"` | 同一品类下已有同名模板，或选项编码重复 | A template with this name already exists for this category type, or two options share the same code | 同一品类下模板重名，或同一模板里两个选项的 code 相同。 |
| `SPEC_VALUE_NEEDS_QUANTITY` | `80009, "err.spec_value.needs_quantity"` | 这一档要写清数量，例如 750g 或 1.5kg | Include the amount, e.g. 750g or 1.5kg | 量纲维度（QUANT）下新加的档位里抽不出数量。 |
| `NOTIFY_WX_QUOTA_EMPTY` | `80101, "err.notify.wx_quota_empty"` | 该用户没有可用的订阅额度，这条测试会白发。请改用有额度的测试账号 | This user has no subscribe-message quota left, so the test would go nowhere. Use a test account that still has quota | 该用户没有可用的微信订阅额度，测试会白发（发出去也会被微信以 43101 拒） |
| `NOTIFY_NO_DEVICE` | `80102, "err.notify.no_device"` | 该用户没有绑定 App 设备：可能没装、没登录过 App，或已登出解绑 | This user has no app device bound: not installed, never signed in on the app, or unbound at sign-out | 该用户没有绑定 App 设备：没装、没登录过 App，或已登出解绑 |
| `TRACK_NOT_RECIPIENT` | `80103, "err.track.not_recipient"` | 这单不是寄给你的，当前手机号与收货人不一致 | This parcel is not addressed to your phone number | 看件：登录手机号与收货号不一致（非本人，TDD-收件人物流触达 §3）。 |

### 2.3 端上取值域（260）

`登记` 一列取自 `packages/shared/src/contract/enum-registry.ts` ——
端上每个**具名枚举**都必须在那张表里有一条（G1 守卫）。三种取值：

- 登记表里的 `verdict`（`OK` / `MERGE` / `PLANNED` …）
- **未登记** —— 够 G1 判据却不在表里，这一档应当恒为 0
- `不适用` —— 够不上「具名枚举」：单取值，或取值不是大写 token
  （`ROUTES` 是路由表、`FONT_FAMILY` 是字体名、`TRADE_RULES` 是 `"21:00"`）。
  它们仍列在这里 —— 它们确实是常量对象，只是不该被要求登记。

> 本表的扫描面比 G1 宽：G1 在两端只扫 `src/api`，这里扫整个 `src`。
> 所以 **未登记** 这一档同时兼着「G1 的扫描面有没有漏」的探针。
